## 點統計 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 參數

**id**　b365c45a224510a5f3-5

**english**　Point Statistics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 鄰域分析工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html

**parameter**　field

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 欄位
- 將要計算指定統計資料的欄位。 它可以是輸入點圖徵的任何數值欄位。 如果輸入圖徵包含 z 值，則它可以是 Shape 欄位。
- Field

## 點統計 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 參數

**id**　b365c45a224510a5f3-7

**english**　Point Statistics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 鄰域分析工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html

**parameter**　neighborhood

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 鄰域 （可選）
- 每個處理像元周圍的區域，在該區域中找到的所有輸入點均將用于統計計算。 有多種預定義鄰域類型可供選擇。 選擇鄰域類型后，可設置其他參數來完全定義形狀、大小和測量單位。 默認鄰域是寬和高為三個像元的方矩形。 以下為可用鄰域類型的形式： 環形，內半徑，外半徑，單位類型 由內半徑和外半徑定義的環或圓環形鄰域。 半徑的最小值為 1 個像元，并且外半徑必須大于內半徑。 最大內半徑為 2046 個像元，最大外半徑為 2047 個像元。 默認環形的內半徑為 1 個像元，外半徑為 3 個像元。 圓形，半徑，單位類型 具有給定半徑的圓形鄰域。 最小半徑為 1 個像元，最大值為 2047 個像元。 默認半徑為 3 個像元。 矩形，高度，寬度，單位類型 由寬度和高度定義的矩形鄰域。 寬度或高度的最小值為 1 個像元，最大值為 4096 個像元。 默認設置是寬度和高度為 3 個像元的正方形。 楔形，半徑，起始角度，終止角度，單位類型 由半徑、起始角度和終止角度定義的楔形鄰域。 最小半徑為 1 個像元，最大值為 2047 個像元。 楔形按逆時針方向從起始角延伸到終止角。 角度以度為單位進行指定，0 或 360 的值表示東方。 也可使用負角度。 默認楔形起始角度為 0 度，終止角度為 90 度，半徑為 3 個像元。 參數的距離單位可指定為像元單位或地圖單位。 默認設置為“像元”單位。
- Neighborhood

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html#parameters

## 點統計 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 參數

**id**　b365c45a224510a5f3-8

**english**　Point Statistics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 鄰域分析工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html

**parameter**　statistics_type

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 統計類型 （可選）
- 用于指定要計算的統計資料類型。 對位于每個輸出網格像元指定鄰域中的點對應的指定欄位值執行計算。 統計類型的可用選擇取決于指定欄位的數值型。 如果欄位為整型，則所有統計類型均可用。 如果欄位為浮點型，則僅可使用最大值、平均值、最小值、范圍、標準差以及總和。 平均值—將計算每個鄰域中欄位值的平均值。 這是默認設置。 多數—將識別每個鄰域中出現頻率最高的欄位值。 如果出現頻率相同，則使用較低的值。 最大值—將識別每個鄰域中的最大欄位值。 中值—將計算每個鄰域中的中間欄位值。 如果鄰域中的點數為偶數，則結果將為兩個中間值中的較小值。 最小值—將識別每個鄰域中的最小欄位值。 少數—將識別每個鄰域中出現頻率最低的欄位值。 如果出現頻率相同，則使用較低的值。 范圍—將計算每個鄰域內欄位值的范圍（最大值和最小值之差）。 標準差—將計算每個鄰域中欄位值的標準差。 總和—將計算鄰域內欄位值的總和。 變異度—將計算每個鄰域中唯一欄位值的數量。
- String

## 點統計 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 返回值

**id**　b365c45a224510a5f3-14

**english**　Point Statistics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 鄰域分析工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html#return-value

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html

**parameter**　neighborhood

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- neighborhood （可選）
- 每個處理像元周圍的區域，在該區域中找到的所有輸入點均將用于統計計算。 有多種預定義鄰域類型可供選擇。 選擇鄰域類型后，可設置其他參數來完全定義形狀、大小和測量單位。 默認鄰域是寬和高為三個像元的方矩形。 每個輸入點周圍鄰域的形狀將由 Neighborhood 類進行定義。 可用的鄰域類型有 NbrAnnulus、NbrCircle、NbrRectangle , 和 NbrWedge。 以下為可用鄰域類型的形式： NbrAnnulus({innerRadius}, {outerRadius}, {units}) 由內半徑和外半徑定義的環或圓環形鄰域。 半徑的最小值為 1 個像元，并且外半徑必須大于內半徑。 最大內半徑為 2046 個像元，最大外半徑為 2047 個像元。 默認環形的內半徑為 1 個像元，外半徑為 3 個像元。 NbrCircle({radius}, {units} 具有給定半徑的圓形鄰域。 最小半徑為 1 個像元，最大值為 2047 個像元。 默認半徑為 3 個像元。 NbrRectangle({width}, {height}, {units}) 由寬度和高度定義的矩形鄰域。 寬度或高度的最小值為 1 個像元，最大值為 4096 個像元。 默認設置是寬度和高度為 3 個像元的正方形。 NbrWedge({radius}, {startAngle}, {endAngle}, {units}) 由半徑、起始角度和終止角度定義的楔形鄰域。 最小半徑為 1 個像元，最大值為 2047 個像元。 楔形按逆時針方向從起始角延伸到終止角。 角度以度為單位進行指定，0 或 360 的值表示東方。 也可使用負角度。 默認楔形起始角度為 0 度，終止角度為 90 度，半徑為 3 個像元。 參數的距離單位可指定為 CELL 單位或 MAP 單位。 默認設置為“像元”單位。 默認鄰域類型為高和寬為三個像元的 NbrRectangle。
- Neighborhood

**source_language**　zh-cn

## 點統計 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 返回值

**id**　b365c45a224510a5f3-15

**english**　Point Statistics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html#return-value

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html

**parameter**　statistics_type

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- statistics_type （可選）
- 用于指定要計算的統計資料類型。 對位于每個輸出網格像元指定鄰域中的點對應的指定欄位值執行計算。 統計類型的可用選擇取決于指定欄位的數值型。 如果欄位為整型，則所有統計類型均可用。 如果欄位為浮點型，則僅可使用最大值、平均值、最小值、范圍、標準差以及總和。 MEAN—將計算每個鄰域中欄位值的平均值。 這是默認設置。 MAJORITY—將識別每個鄰域中出現頻率最高的欄位值。 如果出現頻率相同，則使用較低的值。 MAXIMUM—將識別每個鄰域中的最大欄位值。 MEDIAN—將計算每個鄰域中的中間欄位值。 如果鄰域中的點數為偶數，則結果將為兩個中間值中的較小值。 MINIMUM—將識別每個鄰域中的最小欄位值。 MINORITY—將識別每個鄰域中出現頻率最低的欄位值。 如果出現頻率相同，則使用較低的值。 RANGE—將計算每個鄰域內欄位值的范圍（最大值和最小值之差）。 STD—將計算每個鄰域中欄位值的標準差。 SUM—將計算鄰域內欄位值的總和。 VARIETY—將計算每個鄰域中唯一欄位值的數量。
- String

**id**　b365c45a224510a5f3-18

**english**　Point Statistics

**text**　import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.sa import *
env.workspace = "C:/sapyexamples/data"
outPointStats = PointStatistics("ca_ozone_pts.shp", "OZONE", 500,
                                NbrCircle(10000, "MAP"), "SUM")
outPointStats.save("C:/sapyexamples/output/pointstatsout")

此示例將為落在每個輸出網格像元周圍的圓形鄰域內的輸入 shapefile 點圖徵確定統計資料（平均值）。

## 點統計 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　b365c45a224510a5f3-20

**english**　Point Statistics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 鄰域分析工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html#return-value

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html

**text**　# Name: PointStatistics_Ex_02.py
# Description: Calculates a statistic on points over a specified
#    neighborhood outputting a raster.
# Requirements: Spatial Analyst extension

# Import system modules
import arcpy
from arcpy import env
from arcpy.sa import *

# Set environment settings
env.workspace = "C:/sapyexamples/data"

# Set local variables
inPointFeatures = "ca_ozone_pts.shp"
field = "OZONE"
cellSize = 500
neighborhood = NbrCircle(6000, "MAP")

# Run PointStatistics
outPointStatistics = PointStatistics(inPointFeatures, field, cellSize,
                                     neighborhood, "MEAN")

# Save the output
outPointStatistics.save("C:/sapyexamples/output/pointstatout")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html#return-value

## 點統計 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 鄰域分析工具集

**english**　Point Statistics

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html

**id**　b365c45a224510a5f3-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Point Statistics，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入點圖徵 [in_point_features]、欄位 [field]、輸出像元大小 （可選） [cell_size]、鄰域 （可選） [neighborhood]、統計類型 （可選） [statistics_type]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html#parameters

## 點統計 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 英文版工具簽章與參數順序

**english**　Point Statistics

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 鄰域分析工具集

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html

**id**　b365c45a224510a5f3-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/point-statistics.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_point_features", "label_zh": "輸入點圖徵", "label_en": "Input point features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input points to use in the neighborhood operation. For each output cell, any input points that fall within the defined neighborhood shape around it are identified. For the selected points, values from the specified attribute are obtained, and a statistic is calculated. The input can be either a point or multipoint feature class."}, {"parameter": "field", "label_zh": "欄位", "label_en": "Field", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field for which the specified statistic will be calculated. It can be any numeric field of the input point features. It can be the Shape field if the input features contain z-values."}, {"parameter": "cell_size", "label_zh": "輸出像元大小 （可選）", "label_en": "Output cell size (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Analysis Cell Size", "description_en": "The cell size of the output raster that will be created. This parameter can be defined by a numeric value or obtained from an existing raster dataset. If the cell size hasn't been explicitly specified as the parameter value, the environment cell size value will be used if specified; otherwise, additional rules will be used to calculate it from the other inputs. See the usage section for more detail."}, {"parameter": "neighborhood", "label_zh": "鄰域 （可選）", "label_en": "Neighborhood (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Neighborhood", "description_en": "The area around each processing cell within which any input points found will be used in the statistics calculation. There are several predefined neighborhood types to choose from. Once the neighborhood type is selected, other parameters can be set to fully define the shape, size, and units of measure. The default neighborhood is a square rectangle with a width and height of three cells. The following are the forms of the available neighborhood types: Annulus, Inner radius, Outer radius, Units type A ring or donut-shaped neighborhood defined by an inner radius and an outer radius. The minimum value for radius is 1 cell, and the outer radius must be larger than the inner. The maximum inner radius is 2046 cells, and the maximum outer radius is 2047 cells. The default annulus is an inner radius of 1 cell and an outer radius of 3 cells. Circle, Radius, Units type A circular neighborhood with the given radius. The minimum value for radius is 1 cell, and the maximum value is 2047 cells. The default radius is 3 cells. Rectangle, Height, Width, Units type A rectangular neighborhood defined by width and height. The minimum value for width or height is 1 cell, and the maximum value is 4096 cells. The default is a square with a width and height of 3 cells. Wedge, Radius, Start angle, End angle, Units type A wedge-shaped neighborhood defined by a radius, a start angle, and an end angle. The minimum value for radius is 1 cell, and the maximum value is 2047 cells. The wedge extends counterclockwise from the starting angle to the ending angle. Angles are specified in degrees, with 0 or 360 representing east. Negative angles can be used. The default wedge is from 0 to 90 degrees, with a radius of 3 cells. The distance units for the parameters can be specified in Cell units or Map units. Cell units is the default."}, {"parameter": "statistics_type", "label_zh": "統計類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Statistics type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the statistic type to be calculated. The calculation is performed on the values of the specified field of the points that fall within the specified neighborhood of each output raster cell. The available choices for the statistic type are determined by the numeric type of the specified field. If the field is integer, all the statistics types will be available. If the field is floating point, only the maximum, mean, minimum, range, standard deviation, and sum statistics will be available. Mean—The average of the field values in each neighborhood will be calculated. This is the default. Majority—The most frequently occurring field value in each neighborhood will be identified. In the case of a tie, the lower value is used. Maximum—The largest field value in each neighborhood will be identified. Median—The median field value in each neighborhood will be calculated. In the case of an even number of points in the neighborhood, the result will be the lower of the two middle values. Minimum—The smallest field value in each neighborhood will be identified. Minority—The least frequently occurring field value in each neighborhood will be identified. In the case of a tie, the lower value is used. Range—The range (the difference between the largest and smallest) of the field values in each neighborhood will be calculated. Standard Deviation—The standard deviation of the field values in each neighborhood will be calculated. Sum—The sum of the field values in the neighborhood will be calculated. Variety—The number of unique field values in each neighborhood will be calculated."}]

**signatures**　
- PointStatistics(in_point_features, field, {cell_size}, {neighborhood}, {statistics_type})
- NbrAnnulus({innerRadius}, {outerRadius}, {units})
- NbrCircle({radius}, {units}
- NbrRectangle({width}, {height}, {units})
- NbrWedge({radius}, {startAngle}, {endAngle}, {units})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_point_features、field、cell_size、neighborhood、statistics_type。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。

工具的名稱

相關資料集詳細信息

我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

## 導出電路 (公共設施網絡工具) · 匯總

**id**　b370c60ab48fd68c93-1

**english**　Export Circuits

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html

**text**　導出電信域網絡 中構成電路的圖徵和對象的圖徵與連通性信息。 資料將以 .json 文件形式保存到指定的輸出文件夾中，每個電路對應一個文件。
導出的資料可用于存檔，或通過 ModelBuilder、腳本或模型工具，或通過與外部第三方系統的集成，作為其他工具的輸入。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#summary

## 導出電路 (公共設施網絡工具) · 用法

**id**　b370c60ab48fd68c93-3

**english**　Export Circuits

**chapter**　
- 工具參考

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html

**text**　輸入公共設施網絡參數值必須引用公共設施網絡版本 8 或更高版本。
使用企業級地理資料庫時，必須滿足以下要求： 輸入公共設施網絡參數值必須來自公共設施網絡服務。 連接的 ArcGIS Enterprise 門戶賬戶必須為門戶公共設施網絡所有者。 許可： 活動門戶賬戶必須分配 ArcGIS Advanced Editing 用戶類型擴展模塊許可，才能在企業部署中使用此工具。
選中設置導出確認參數時，需提供以下信息： 必須啟用 網絡拓撲。 輸入公共設施網絡參數值必須引用默認分支版本。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#usage

- Boolean

- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html

**parameter**　include_up_to_first_spatial_container

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 最多包含第一個空間容器 （可選）
- 指定返回的容器是否僅限于追蹤結果中每個網絡元素的第一個空間容器（包括第一個空間容器）。 如果沒有遇到空間容器，但是存在給定網絡元素的非空間容器，則所有非空間容器都將包含在結果中。 僅當將 include_containers 設置為 INCLUDE_CONTAINERS 時，才會啟用此參數。 選中—當沿追蹤路徑遇到嵌套包含關聯時，結果中僅返回遇到次數最多并包括第一個空間容器的容器。 如果空間容器不存在，則所有給定網絡元素的非空間容器都將包含在結果中。 未選中—所有容器都將在結果中返回。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 驗證可定位性 （可選）
- 指定如果遇到非空間交匯點或邊對象并且其關聯層次結構中沒有必要的連通性、包含或附件關聯，是否在追蹤操作期間返回錯誤。 此參數確保可以通過與圖徵或其他可定位對象 的關聯來定位追蹤或更新子網操作返回的非空間對象。 選中—如果遇到無法定位 的非空間交匯點或邊對象并且其關聯層次結構中沒有必要的連通性、包含或附件關聯，則追蹤將返回一條錯誤。 未選中—追蹤不會檢查是否存在無法定位的對象并返回結果，無論遍歷對象的關聯層次結構中是否存在無法定位的對象。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

## 導出電路 (公共設施網絡工具) · 參數

**id**　b370c60ab48fd68c93-18

**english**　Export Circuits

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html

**parameter**　condition_barriers

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 條件障礙 （可選）
- 基于與網絡屬性的比較或對類別字符串的檢查，對圖徵設置可遍歷性障礙條件。 條件障礙根據網絡屬性或類別、操作符、類型及屬性值來定義。 例如，當圖徵的 Device Status 屬性等于 Open 的特定值時，將停止追蹤。 當圖徵滿足此條件時，追蹤將停止。 如果您要使用多個屬性，可使用組合方法參數來定義 And 或 Or 條件。 了解有關使用多條件表達式的詳細信息 值表列： Name—Filter by network attribute name. Operator—Filter by an operator. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any (bitwise AND equals False)—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Type—Filter by a specific type. Specific value—Filter by a specific value or network category. Network attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Filter by a specific value. Combine Using—Use this parameter to combine multiple condition barriers. And—Combine the condition barriers. Or—Use if either condition barrier is met.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#parameters

## 導出電路 (公共設施網絡工具) · 參數

**id**　b370c60ab48fd68c93-19

**english**　Export Circuits

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html

**parameter**　function_barriers

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 函數障礙 （可選）
- 基于函數對圖徵設置可遍歷性障礙，以限制用于篩選待導出電路的電路追蹤。 函數障礙可用于執行以下操作：限制追蹤距離起點的行程或設置停止追蹤的最大值。 這可用于約束將要導出的電路。 例如，考慮一個場景：您希望導出長度大于 1 千米的電路。 可以使用函數障礙，僅在總長度超過 1,000 米時導出指定電路。 值表列： Function—Filter by a function. Minimum—The minimum of the input values will be identified. Maximum—The maximum of the input values will be identified. Average—The average of the input values will be calculated. Count—The number of features will be identified. Add—The sum of the input values will be calculated. Subtract—The difference between the input values will be calculated. Attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Operator—Filter by an operator. Is equal to—The function result is equal to the value. Does not equal—The function result is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The function result is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The function result is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The function result is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The function result is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any (bitwise AND equals False)—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Value—Filter by a specific value. Use Local Values—Boolean value that uses local variables instead of a global variable.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#parameters

- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 應用可遍歷性至 （可選）
- 指定可遍歷性障礙是否應用于交匯點和/或邊。 例如，如果定義了用于停止追蹤的條件障礙，其中 Device Status 等于 Proposed 并將可遍歷性范圍設置為僅邊，則即使遇到擬建設備，電路追蹤也不會停止，因為 Device Status 僅適用于交匯點。 換言之，此參數會為追蹤指明是否要忽略交匯點和/或邊。 交匯點和邊—可遍歷性將同時應用于交匯點和邊。 這是默認設置。 僅交匯點—可遍歷性將僅應用于交匯點。 僅邊—可遍歷性將僅應用于邊。
- String

- 資料類型

**values**　
- 傳播程序 （可選）
- 指定要傳播的網絡屬性以及傳播將在追蹤過程中的發生方式。 傳播的類屬性表示網絡中各個圖徵上已傳播至電路中其余圖徵的關鍵值。 例如，在電信域網絡中，您可以從定義為電路位置 的圖徵中傳播波長值。 注: 此參數僅可用于 Python。 值表列： Attribute—Filter using any network attribute defined in the system. Substitution Attribute—Substitution is not supported with telecom domain networks. Function—Wavelength propagation supports use of the function PROPAGATED_BITWISE_AND. Operator—Choose from the operators INCLUDES_ANY and DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY for wavelength propagation. Value—Provide a specific value for the input attribute type that would cause termination based on the operator value.
- Value Table

**id**　b370c60ab48fd68c93-38

**english**　Export Circuits

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html

**parameter**　include_up_to_first_spatial_container

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- include_up_to_first_spatial_container （可選）
- 指定返回的容器是否僅限于追蹤結果中每個網絡元素的第一個空間容器（包括第一個空間容器）。 如果沒有遇到空間容器，但是存在給定網絡元素的非空間容器，則所有非空間容器都將包含在結果中。 僅當將 include_containers 設置為 INCLUDE_CONTAINERS 時，才會啟用此參數。 INCLUDE_UP_TO_FIRST_SPATIAL_CONTAINER—當沿追蹤路徑遇到嵌套包含關聯時，結果中僅返回遇到次數最多并包括第一個空間容器的容器。 如果空間容器不存在，則所有給定網絡元素的非空間容器都將包含在結果中。 DO_NOT_INCLUDE_UP_TO_FIRST_SPATIAL_CONTAINER—所有容器都將在結果中返回。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

## 導出電路 (公共設施網絡工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　b370c60ab48fd68c93-43

**english**　Export Circuits

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html

**parameter**　validate_locatability

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- validate_locatability （可選）
- 指定如果遇到非空間交匯點或邊對象并且其關聯層次結構中沒有必要的連通性、包含或附件關聯，是否在追蹤操作期間返回錯誤。 此參數確保可以通過與圖徵或其他可定位對象 的關聯來定位追蹤或更新子網操作返回的非空間對象。 VALIDATE_LOCATABILITY—如果遇到無法定位 的非空間交匯點或邊對象并且其關聯層次結構中沒有必要的連通性、包含或附件關聯，則追蹤將返回一條錯誤。 DO_NOT_VALIDATE_LOCATABILITY—追蹤不會檢查是否存在無法定位的對象并返回結果，無論遍歷對象的關聯層次結構中是否存在無法定位的對象。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#derived-output

## 導出電路 (公共設施網絡工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　b370c60ab48fd68c93-45

**english**　Export Circuits

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html

**parameter**　condition_barriers

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- condition_barriers [[Name, Operator, Type, Value, Combine Using],...] （可選）
- 基于與網絡屬性的比較或對類別字符串的檢查，對圖徵設置可遍歷性障礙條件。 條件障礙根據網絡屬性或類別、操作符、類型及屬性值來定義。 例如，當圖徵的 Device Status 屬性等于 Open 的特定值時，將停止追蹤。 當圖徵滿足此條件時，追蹤將停止。 如果您要使用多個屬性，可使用組合方法參數來定義 And 或 Or 條件。 了解有關使用多條件表達式的詳細信息 值表列： Name—Filter by network attribute name. Operator—Filter by an operator. IS_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is equal to the value. DOES_NOT_EQUAL—The attribute is not equal to the value. IS_GREATER_THAN—The attribute is greater than the value. IS_GREATER_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. IS_LESS_THAN—The attribute is less than the value. IS_LESS_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. INCLUDES_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). INCLUDES_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Type—Filter by a specific type. SPECIFIC_VALUE—Filter by a specific value or network category. NETWORK_ATTRIBUTE—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Filter by a specific value. Combine Using—Use this parameter to combine multiple condition barriers. AND—Combine the condition barriers. OR—Use if either condition barrier is met.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#derived-output

## 導出電路 (公共設施網絡工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　b370c60ab48fd68c93-46

**english**　Export Circuits

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html

**parameter**　function_barriers

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- function_barriers [[Function, Attribute, Operator, Value, Use Local Values],...] （可選）
- 基于函數對圖徵設置可遍歷性障礙，以限制用于篩選待導出電路的電路追蹤。 函數障礙可用于執行以下操作：限制追蹤距離起點的行程或設置停止追蹤的最大值。 這可用于約束將要導出的電路。 例如，考慮一個場景：您希望導出長度大于 1 千米的電路。 可以使用函數障礙，僅在總長度超過 1,000 米時導出電路。 函數障礙 Function 值選項如下： 值表列： Function—Filter by a function. MIN—The minimum of the input values will be identified. MAX—The maximum of the input values will be identified. AVERAGE—The average of the input values will be calculated. COUNT—The number of features will be identified. ADD—The sum of the input values will be calculated. SUBTRACT—The difference between the input values will be calculated. Attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Operator—Filter by an operator. IS_EQUAL_TO—The function result is equal to the value. DOES_NOT_EQUAL—The function result is not equal to the value. IS_GREATER_THAN—The function result is greater than the value. IS_GREATER_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The function result is greater than or equal to the value. IS_LESS_THAN—The function result is less than the value. IS_LESS_THAN_OR_EQUAL_TO—The function result is less than or equal to the value. INCLUDES_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_THE_VALUES—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). INCLUDES_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Value—Filter by a specific value. Use Local Values—Boolean value that uses local variables instead of a global variable.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#derived-output

**english**　Export Circuits

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html

**parameter**　traversability_scope

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- traversability_scope （可選）
- 指定可遍歷性障礙是否應用于交匯點和/或邊。 例如，如果定義了用于停止追蹤的條件障礙，其中 Device Status 等于 Proposed 并將可遍歷性范圍設置為僅邊，則即使遇到擬建設備，電路追蹤也不會停止，因為 Device Status 僅適用于交匯點。 換言之，此參數會為追蹤指明是否要忽略交匯點和/或邊。 BOTH_JUNCTIONS_AND_EDGES—可遍歷性將同時應用于交匯點和邊。 這是默認設置。 JUNCTIONS_ONLY—可遍歷性將僅應用于交匯點。 EDGES_ONLY—可遍歷性將僅應用于邊。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#derived-output

**english**　Export Circuits

**parameter**　propagators

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- propagators [[Attribute, Substitution Attribute, Function, Operator, Value],...] （可選）
- 指定要傳播的網絡屬性以及傳播將在追蹤過程中的發生方式。 傳播的類屬性表示網絡中各個圖徵上已傳播至電路中其余圖徵的關鍵值。 例如，在電信域網絡中，您可以從定義為電路位置 的圖徵中傳播波長值。 注: 此參數僅可用于 Python。 值表列： Attribute—Filter using any network attribute defined in the system. Substitution Attribute—Substitution is not supported with telecom domain networks. Function—Wavelength propagation supports use of the function PROPAGATED_BITWISE_AND. Operator—Choose from the operators INCLUDES_ANY and DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY for wavelength propagation. Value—Provide a specific value for the input attribute type that would cause termination based on the operator value.
- Value Table

## 導出電路 (公共設施網絡工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱

**english**　Export Circuits

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html

**id**　b370c60ab48fd68c93-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Export Circuits，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入公共設施網絡 [in_utility_network]、域網絡 [domain_network]、設置導出確認 [export_acknowledged]、輸出文件夾 [out_folder_path]、最大跳數 （可選） [max_hops]、包括容器 （可選） [include_containers]、最多包含第一個空間容器 （可選） [include_up_to_first_spatial_container]、包括內容 （可選） [include_content]、包括結構 （可選） [include_structures]、包括障礙圖徵 （可選） [include_barriers]、忽略起點處障礙 （可選） [ignore_barriers_at_starting_points]、驗證可定位性 （可選） [validate_locatability]、在服務器上以異步模式運行 （可選） [run_async]、條件障礙 （可選） [condition_barriers]、函數障礙 （可選） [function_barriers]、應用可遍歷性至 （可選） [traversability_scope]、傳播程序 （可選） [propagators]、包括幾何 （可選） [include_geometry]、包括域描述 （可選） [include_domain_descriptions]、合成幾何 （可選） [synthesize_geometries]、結果類型 （可選） [result_types]、結果網絡屬性 （可選） [result_network_attributes]、結果欄位 （可選） [result_fields]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#parameters

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/export-circuits.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_utility_network", "label_zh": "輸入公共設施網絡", "label_en": "Input Utility Network", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Utility Network; Utility Network Layer", "description_en": "The utility network that contains the circuits that will be exported."}, {"parameter": "domain_network", "label_zh": "域網絡", "label_en": "Domain Network", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The telecom domain network that contains the circuits that will be exported."}, {"parameter": "circuits", "label_zh": "", "label_en": "Circuits", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the circuit or circuits that will be exported. Use a comma-delimited list of circuit names for multiple circuits."}, {"parameter": "export_acknowledged", "label_zh": "設置導出確認", "label_en": "Set export acknowledged", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the LASTEXPORTED attribute for the corresponding circuit in the Circuit table will be updated. Checked—The LASTEXPORTED attribute for the corresponding circuit in the Circuit table will be updated. If the circuit has been marked for deletion (Status = Deleted), it will be removed from the utility network. The Input Utility Network parameter must reference the default version to use this option. Unchecked—The LASTEXPORTED attribute for the corresponding circuit in the Circuit table will not be updated. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "out_folder_path", "label_zh": "輸出文件夾", "label_en": "Output folder", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Folder", "description_en": "The name and location of the output folder that will store the .json files created for each exported circuit."}, {"parameter": "max_hops", "label_zh": "最大跳數 （可選）", "label_en": "Max Hops (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "The maximum number of hops between edges allowed in the path, excluding the start location. All edges and junction-junction connectivity associations are evaluated as having a fixed unit length of 1 on traversal. The default is 100."}, {"parameter": "include_containers", "label_zh": "包括容器 （可選）", "label_en": "Include containers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the container features will be included in the trace results. Checked—Container features will be included in the trace results. Unchecked—Container features will not be included in the trace results. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "include_up_to_first_spatial_container", "label_zh": "最多包含第一個空間容器 （可選）", "label_en": "Include up to first spatial container (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the containers returned will be limited to those encountered up to, and including, the first spatial container for each network element in the trace results. If no spatial containers are encountered but nonspatial containers are present for a given network element, all nonspatial containers will be included in the results. This parameter is only enabled when include_containers is set to INCLUDE_CONTAINERS. Checked—Only containers encountered up to, and including, the first spatial container will be returned in the results when nested containment associations are encountered along the trace path. If no spatial containers exist, all nonspatial containers will be included in the results for a given network element. Unchecked—All containers will be returned in the results. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "include_content", "label_zh": "包括內容 （可選）", "label_en": "Include content (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the trace will return content in container features in the results. Checked—Content in container features will be included in the trace results. Unchecked—Content in container features will not be included in the trace results. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "include_structures", "label_zh": "包括結構 （可選）", "label_en": "Include structures (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether structure features and objects will be included in the trace results. Checked—Structure features and objects will be included in the trace results. Unchecked—Structure features and objects will not be included in the trace results. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "include_barriers", "label_zh": "包括障礙圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Include barrier features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the traversability barrier features will be included in the trace results. Traversability barriers are optional even if they have been preset in the subnetwork definition. This parameter does not apply to device features with terminals. Checked—Traversability barrier features will be included in the trace results. This is the default. Unchecked—Traversability barrier features will not be included in the trace results."}, {"parameter": "ignore_barriers_at_starting_points", "label_zh": "忽略起點處障礙 （可選）", "label_en": "Ignore barriers at starting points (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether dynamic barriers in the trace configuration will be ignored for starting points. Checked—Barriers at starting points will be ignored in the trace. Unchecked—Barriers at starting points will not be ignored in the trace. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "validate_locatability", "label_zh": "驗證可定位性 （可選）", "label_en": "Validate locatability (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether an error will be returned during a trace if nonspatial junction or edge objects are encountered without the necessary connectivity, containment, or attachment association in their association hierarchy. This parameter ensures that nonspatial objects returned by a trace or update subnetwork operation can be located through an association with features or other locatable objects. Checked—The trace will return an error if unlocatable nonspatial junction or edge objects are encountered without the necessary connectivity, containment, or attachment association in their association hierarchy. Unchecked—The trace will not check for unlocatable objects and will return results regardless of whether unlocatable objects are present in the association hierarchy of the traversed objects. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "run_async", "label_zh": "在服務器上以異步模式運行 （可選）", "label_en": "Run in asynchronous mode on the server (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the export operation will process asynchronously when working with a utility network in an enterprise geodatabase. Checked—Export operations will process asynchronously. Unchecked—Export operations will process synchronously. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "condition_barriers", "label_zh": "條件障礙 （可選）", "label_en": "Condition Barriers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Sets a traversability barrier condition on features based on a comparison to a network attribute or check for a category string. A condition barrier uses a network attribute or network category, an operator and a type, and an attribute value. For example, stop a trace when a feature has the Device Status attribute equal to the specific value of Open. When a feature meets this condition, the trace stops. If you're using more than one attribute, you can use the Combine using parameter to define an And or an Or condition. Learn more about using multiple conditional expressions Value table columns: Name—Filter by network attribute name. Operator—Filter by an operator. Is equal to—The attribute is equal to the value. Does not equal—The attribute is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The attribute is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The attribute is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The attribute is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The attribute is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any (bitwise AND equals False)—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Type—Filter by a specific type. Specific value—Filter by a specific value or network category. Network attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Value—Filter by a specific value. Combine Using—Use this parameter to combine multiple condition barriers. And—Combine the condition barriers. Or—Use if either condition barrier is met."}, {"parameter": "function_barriers", "label_zh": "函數障礙 （可選）", "label_en": "Function Barriers (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Sets a traversability barrier on features based on a function to restrict the circuit trace used to filter the circuits that will be exported. Function barriers can be used to do such things as restrict how far the trace travels from the starting point, or set a maximum value to stop a trace. This can be used to constrain the circuits that will be exported. For example, consider a scenario where you want to export circuits with a length greater than 1 kilometer. A function barrier could be used to only export the specified circuits when the total length exceeds 1,000 meters. Value table columns: Function—Filter by a function. Minimum—The minimum of the input values will be identified. Maximum—The maximum of the input values will be identified. Average—The average of the input values will be calculated. Count—The number of features will be identified. Add—The sum of the input values will be calculated. Subtract—The difference between the input values will be calculated. Attribute—Filter by a network attribute. Operator—Filter by an operator. Is equal to—The function result is equal to the value. Does not equal—The function result is not equal to the value. Is greater than—The function result is greater than the value. Is greater than or equal to—The function result is greater than or equal to the value. Is less than—The function result is less than the value. Is less than or equal to—The function result is less than or equal to the value. Includes the values (bitwise AND equals the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which all bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == value). Does not include the values (bitwise AND does not equal the value)—A bitwise AND operation in which not all of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND != value). Includes any (bitwise AND equals True)—A bitwise AND operation in which at least one bit in the value is present in the attribute (bitwise AND == True). Does not include any (bitwise AND equals False)—A bitwise AND operation in which none of the bits in the value are present in the attribute (bitwise AND == False). Value—Filter by a specific value. Use local values—Boolean value that uses local variables instead of a global variable."}, {"parameter": "traversability_scope", "label_zh": "應用可遍歷性至 （可選）", "label_en": "Apply Traversability To (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether traversability barriers will be applied to junctions, edges, or both. For example, if a condition barrier is defined to stop the trace if Device Status is equal to Proposed and traversability scope is set to edges only, the circuit trace will not stop—even if it encounters a proposed device—because Device Status is only applicable to junctions. In other words, this parameter indicates to the trace whether to ignore junctions, edges, or both. Both junctions and edges—Traversability will be applied to both junctions and edges. This is the default. Junctions only—Traversability will be applied to junctions only. Edges only—Traversability will be applied to edges only."}, {"parameter": "propagators", "label_zh": "傳播程序 （可選）", "label_en": "Propagators (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Specifies the network attributes to propagate as well as how that propagation will occur during a trace. Propagated class attributes denote the key values on features in a network that are disseminated to the rest of the features in the circuit. For example, in a telecom domain network, you can propagate wavelengths values from features defined as circuit locations. Note: This parameter is only available for Python. Value table columns: Attribute—Filter using any network attribute defined in the system. Substitution Attribute—Substitution is not supported with telecom domain networks. Function—Wavelength propagation supports use of the function PROPAGATED_BITWISE_AND. Operator—Choose from the operators INCLUDES_ANY and DOES_NOT_INCLUDE_ANY for wavelength propagation. Value—Provide a specific value for the input attribute type that would cause termination based on the operator value."}, {"parameter": "include_geometry", "label_zh": "包括幾何 （可選）", "label_en": "Include geometry (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether geometry will be included in the results. Checked—Geometry will be included in the results. Unchecked—Geometry will not be included in the results. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "include_domain_descriptions", "label_zh": "包括域描述 （可選）", "label_en": "Include domain descriptions (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether domain descriptions will be included in the output .json file to communicate domain mapping for circuits. This parameter does not apply to the circuits result types option. Checked—Domain descriptions will be included in the results. Unchecked—Domain descriptions will not be included in the results. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "synthesize_geometries", "label_zh": "合成幾何 （可選）", "label_en": "Synthesize geometries (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether geometries will be inferred and created (synthesized) for nonspatial junction and edge objects traversed as part of a circuit trace when using the circuits result types option. The include_geometry parameter must also be true to include geometry in the output .json file. Checked—Geometries will be inferred and created for the traversed nonspatial junction and edge objects in the output .json file. Unchecked—Geometries will not be synthesized for the traversed nonspatial junction and edge objects. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "result_types", "label_zh": "結果類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Result Types (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the types of results that will be returned. Connectivity—Features that are connected through geometric coincidence or connectivity associations will be returned. This is the default. Features—Feature-level information will be returned. This is the default. Containment and attachment associations—Features that are associated through containment and structural attachment associations will be returned. Circuits—The circuit definition will be returned with the option to include path connectivity information and geometry for each section in the circuit."}, {"parameter": "result_network_attributes", "label_zh": "結果網絡屬性 （可選）", "label_en": "Result Network Attributes (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The network attributes that will be included in the results when using the Features result types option."}, {"parameter": "result_fields", "label_zh": "結果欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Result Fields (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "The fields from a feature class that will be returned as results when using the Features result types option. The values of the fields will be returned in the results for the features in the circuit. Value table columns: Feature Class—A feature or object class that participates in the utility network. Field Name—The name of a field from the feature or object class that will have its values returned in the results."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.un.ExportCircuits(in_utility_network, domain_network, circuits, export_acknowledged, out_folder_path, {max_hops}, {include_containers}, {include_up_to_first_spatial_container}, {include_content}, {include_structures}, {include_barriers}, {ignore_barriers_at_starting_points}, {validate_locatability}, {run_async}, {condition_barriers}, {function_barriers}, {traversability_scope}, {propagators}, {include_geometry}, {include_domain_descriptions}, {synthesize_geometries}, {result_types}, {result_network_attributes}, {result_fields})
